on weeks when I'm not especially energized, I can get convinced that calendar glitches or certain comments in staff meetings are signs someone is checking whether I can handle my family situation, and ========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
No, I've never needed to go to a hospital or anything extreme like that. My work has stayed intact overall, but... well, I have noticed I've missed a few calendar details lately. I've had to double-check tasks that used to just be completely automatic for me. 

And in my personal life, I've actually pulled back from my photography outings and the mentoring I usually do. I just don't feel as present or reliable when I'm like that. So it definitely affects things, but not to the point where I can't function or need to be hospitalized.
